# ESLint 10.11.0

Upstream: [ESLint 10.11.0](https://github.com/eslint/eslint/tree/v10.11.0) (commit `3c0b7c6e1fe2dec778b97a996018126f22d437ae`). License: MIT (Copyright OpenJS Foundation and other contributors, <www.openjsf.org>); [`LICENSE`](LICENSE) is the upstream license file, copied verbatim at that tag.

The files listed below contain material copied from ESLint and remain under its license. Every other file in this
pack is Rerail's. The rule documents in `rules/` and `candidates/rules/` are Rerail's re-implementations of ESLint's
rules (and, for `candidates/rules/unicorn-*.json`, of eslint-plugin-unicorn's, from which no file is copied), published
under the pack's licence expression, `Apache-2.0 AND MIT`, as `REUSE.toml` annotates them. The rest is under the
Apache License 2.0 (the pack's `LICENSE`): the recorder `upstream/generate.mjs`, and the `*.expected.json` recordings
it writes by running ESLint.

Each copied file is the `code` of one of the rule's own `valid` or `invalid` RuleTester cases, verbatim, written
by `upstream/generate.mjs`: cases with a custom parser or type information, and performance stress cases over 64 KiB,
are left out, and identical code in the same language is written once.

Paths are relative to the pack directory, `packs/javascript/`.
